=== lib/features/onboarding/onboarding_pages.dart (onboarding) ===
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:my_app/core/l10n/l10n.dart';

/// The pages of the onboarding, in the order in which the user goes through
/// them: a welcome with the name of the app, and a page that ends it.
///
/// To change what the onboarding shows, change this list. A page is any
/// widget, such as an [OnboardingPage]. The screen of the onboarding shows
/// them one at a time, with Skip above them and, below them, a mark for
/// each page and the button that leads on. Before the last page of the
/// list, it shows the steps that the modules of the app add to the
/// onboarding, each on an [OnboardingPage] with the icon of the step.
List<Widget> onboardingPages(BuildContext context) => [
  OnboardingPage(
    symbol: 'Ma',
    title: 'My App',
    text: context.l10n.onboardingWelcome,
  ),
  OnboardingPage(
    icon: Icons.check_rounded,
    title: context.l10n.onboardingReadyTitle,
    text: context.l10n.onboardingReady,
  ),
];

/// What a page of the onboarding knows of the pages around it: its place
/// among them, and the controller that tells how far the user has turned
/// them. The screen of the onboarding puts one around each page, so that
/// the parts of a page can move at their own pace while the pages turn.
class OnboardingPageScope extends InheritedWidget {
  /// Creates the scope of the page at [index].
  const OnboardingPageScope({
    required this.index,
    required this.controller,
    required super.child,
    super.key,
  });

  /// The place of the page among the pages, from zero.
  final int index;

  /// The controller of the pages.
  final PageController controller;

  /// The scope of the page around [context], or `null` for a page that is
  /// shown outside the screen of the onboarding.
  static OnboardingPageScope? maybeOf(BuildContext context) =>
      context.dependOnInheritedWidgetOfExactType<OnboardingPageScope>();

  /// How far the user has turned the pages of [controller], in pages: zero
  /// on the first page, one on the second, and a fraction in between while
  /// they turn. Before the pages are first laid out, it is the page that
  /// they start on.
  ///
  /// The controller knows its page from the first layout on, also right
  /// after its pages got a new scroll position, as when the language of the
  /// device changes: a page that is built then stays where it is.
  static double turnedOf(PageController controller) =>
      (controller.hasClients ? controller.page : null) ??
      controller.initialPage.toDouble();

  /// How far the pages are turned away from this page: zero while it is the
  /// one on the screen, up to one once the user has turned on to the next
  /// page, and down to minus one before the user has reached it.
  double get turned => (turnedOf(controller) - index).clamp(-1, 1);

  @override
  bool updateShouldNotify(OnboardingPageScope oldWidget) =>
      oldWidget.index != index || oldWidget.controller != controller;
}

/// A page of the onboarding: a cell of the periodic table with [symbol] or
/// [icon], among smaller cells, and a title and a text below them. The
/// cells and the texts move at their own pace while the pages turn. The
/// page scrolls when it is too small for them, as with a large text size on
/// a small phone, where the texts keep their words whole. A screen reader
/// announces the title as a header, and passes over the cells, which are a
/// picture.
class OnboardingPage extends StatelessWidget {
  /// Creates the page.
  const OnboardingPage({
    required this.title,
    required this.text,
    this.symbol,
    this.icon,
    super.key,
  }) : assert(symbol != null || icon != null, 'A page shows one of them.');

  /// The letters in the cell of the page, as an element has a symbol.
  final String? symbol;

  /// The icon in the cell of the page, when it has no [symbol].
  final IconData? icon;

  /// The title of the page.
  final String title;

  /// What the page says below its title.
  final String text;

  /// How far the texts keep from the sides of the page.
  static const double _sides = 28;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final scope = OnboardingPageScope.maybeOf(context);
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        // The texts, built once for a width and not on each frame while the
        // pages turn, since measuring their words takes time.
        final texts = Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Semantics(
              header: true,
              // The title is large already, so it grows only by half with
              // the text size of the device.
              child: OnboardingWords(
                title,
                style: theme.textTheme.displaySmall,
                maxScaleFactor: 1.5,
                width: constraints.maxWidth - 2 * _sides,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            OnboardingWords(
              text,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
                fontSize: 18,
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
              ),
              width: constraints.maxWidth - 2 * _sides,
            ),
          ],
        );
        if (scope == null) {
          return _build(constraints, texts, turned: 0, index: 0);
        }
        return AnimatedBuilder(
          animation: scope.controller,
          child: texts,
          builder: (context, texts) => _build(
            constraints,
            texts!,
            turned: scope.turned,
            index: scope.index,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  /// The page at [index] in [constraints], with [texts] below its cells,
  /// while the pages are [turned] away from it.
  Widget _build(
    BoxConstraints constraints,
    Widget texts, {
    required double turned,
    required int index,
  }) {
    // The texts leave faster than the page, and fade as they go.
    final shown = (1 - turned.abs() * 1.6).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: BoxConstraints(minHeight: constraints.maxHeight),
        child: IntrinsicHeight(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: ConstrainedBox(
                  constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 280),
                  child: ExcludeSemantics(
                    child: _Cells(
                      mirrored: index.isOdd,
                      turned: turned,
                      number: index + 1,
                      symbol: symbol,
                      icon: icon,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(_sides, 8, _sides, 8),
                child: Opacity(
                  opacity: shown,
                  child: Transform.translate(
                    offset: Offset(-turned * 56, 0),
                    child: texts,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A text that keeps its words whole: it breaks between its words and never
/// inside one.
///
/// It grows with the text size of the device, by [maxScaleFactor] at most.
/// Where its longest word would still not fit the width that the text has,
/// as on a narrow phone with a large text size, the text is as large as
/// that word allows.
class OnboardingWords extends StatelessWidget {
  /// Creates the text.
  const OnboardingWords(
    this.text, {
    this.style,
    this.maxScaleFactor = double.infinity,
    this.width,
    super.key,
  });

  /// The text.
  final String text;

  /// The style of the text, over that of the text around it. With none,
  /// the text is as the text around it, as the label of a button is in the
  /// style that the theme of the app gives the button.
  final TextStyle? style;

  /// By how much the text grows at most with the text size of the device.
  final double maxScaleFactor;

  /// The width that the text has. Without it, the text takes the width
  /// that its parent gives it, which a parent that asks for the size of
  /// its children before it lays them out, such as [IntrinsicHeight], does
  /// not tell: below such a parent, the text needs it.
  final double? width;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (width case final width?) return _within(context, width);
    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) => _within(context, constraints.maxWidth),
    );
  }

  /// The text, as large as its words allow in the width [room].
  Widget _within(BuildContext context, double room) {
    // The style in which a Text paints: over that of the text around it,
    // and bold for a user who asks for bold text.
    var style = DefaultTextStyle.of(context).style.merge(this.style);
    if (MediaQuery.boldTextOf(context)) {
      style = style.merge(const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold));
    }
    final scaler = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(
      context,
    ).clamp(maxScaleFactor: maxScaleFactor);
    final painter = TextPainter(
      textDirection: Directionality.of(context),
      textScaler: scaler,
      maxLines: 1,
    );
    // The words of the text, between its white spaces, where a line may
    // break: every one but a no-break space, which keeps the words around
    // it together.
    final words = text.split(RegExp(r'[^\S\u00A0\u2007\u202F]+'));
    // How wide the longest word is in a font of [size].
    double widestAt(double size) {
      var widest = 0.0;
      for (final word in words) {
        painter
          ..text = TextSpan(
            text: word,
            style: style.copyWith(fontSize: size),
          )
          ..layout();
        if (painter.width > widest) widest = painter.width;
      }
      return widest;
    }

    // The size of a text of Flutter whose style gives none.
    var size = style.fontSize ?? 14;
    var widest = widestAt(size);
    // In a few steps: the text size of a device may grow a small font by
    // more than a large one.
    for (var step = 0; step < 4 && widest > room && room > 0; step++) {
      // A little smaller than the width allows, so that the rounding of the
      // layout breaks no word.
      size *= room / widest * 0.98;
      widest = widestAt(size);
    }
    painter.dispose();
    return Text(
      text,
      style: style.copyWith(fontSize: size),
      textScaler: scaler,
    );
  }
}

/// The picture of a page: the cell of the page in the middle, over a grid
/// that fades at its edges, with smaller cells around it. They come in
/// once, when the page is first shown, and each moves at its own pace while
/// the pages turn.
class _Cells extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Cells({
    required this.turned,
    required this.number,
    required this.mirrored,
    this.symbol,
    this.icon,
  });

  /// How far the pages are turned away from the page of the picture.
  final double turned;

  /// The number in the corner of the cell of the page.
  final int number;

  /// Whether the smaller cells are on the other side, as on every other
  /// page, so that two pages in a row do not look the same.
  final bool mirrored;

  final String? symbol;
  final IconData? icon;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    // The smaller cells: where each is, its size, its colour, whether it is
    // only an outline, and how far it moves while the page turns.
    final around = [
      (
        at: const Alignment(-0.74, -0.50),
        size: 60.0,
        color: colors.secondaryContainer,
        outlined: false,
        travel: 120.0,
      ),
      (
        at: const Alignment(0.80, -0.70),
        size: 44.0,
        color: colors.outline,
        outlined: true,
        travel: 190.0,
      ),
      (
        at: const Alignment(0.72, 0.56),
        size: 68.0,
        color: colors.primaryContainer,
        outlined: false,
        travel: 90.0,
      ),
      (
        at: const Alignment(-0.60, 0.70),
        size: 40.0,
        color: colors.tertiaryContainer,
        outlined: false,
        travel: 160.0,
      ),
      (
        at: const Alignment(-0.08, -0.90),
        size: 30.0,
        color: colors.outline,
        outlined: true,
        travel: 230.0,
      ),
      (
        at: const Alignment(0.22, 0.92),
        size: 26.0,
        color: colors.secondary,
        outlined: false,
        travel: 250.0,
      ),
    ];
    // The smaller cells fade as the page leaves.
    final leaving = (1 - turned.abs()).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    return TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
      tween: Tween(begin: 0, end: 1),
      // An app that asks for less motion shows the picture at once.
      duration: MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
          ? Duration.zero
          : const Duration(milliseconds: 1000),
      builder: (context, entered, _) => Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        children: [
          Positioned.fill(
            child: Opacity(
              opacity: Curves.easeOut.transform(entered),
              child: ShaderMask(
                blendMode: BlendMode.dstIn,
                shaderCallback: (bounds) => const RadialGradient(
                  radius: 0.52,
                  colors: [Colors.white, Colors.transparent],
                  stops: [0.25, 1],
                ).createShader(bounds),
                child: CustomPaint(
                  painter: _GridPainter(
                    color: colors.outlineVariant,
                    shift: -turned * 36,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          for (final (order, cell) in around.indexed)
            Align(
              alignment: mirrored ? Alignment(-cell.at.x, cell.at.y) : cell.at,
              child: _entering(
                entered,
                order: order + 1,
                opacity: leaving,
                offset: Offset(-turned * cell.travel, 0),
                child: Container(
                  width: cell.size,
                  height: cell.size,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: cell.outlined ? null : cell.color,
                    border: cell.outlined
                        ? Border.all(color: cell.color)
                        : null,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          _entering(
            entered,
            order: 0,
            opacity: 1,
            offset: Offset(-turned * 28, 0),
            scale: 1 - turned.abs() * 0.14,
            angle: turned * 0.07,
            child: _Cell(number: number, symbol: symbol, icon: icon),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  /// [child] as it comes in while [entered] grows, later for a higher
  /// [order]: it fades in and settles from below and from a smaller size.
  Widget _entering(
    double entered, {
    required int order,
    required double opacity,
    required Offset offset,
    required Widget child,
    double scale = 1,
    double angle = 0,
  }) {
    final start = order * 0.09;
    final settled = Interval(
      start,
      (start + 0.5).clamp(0, 1),
      curve: Easing.emphasizedDecelerate,
    ).transform(entered);
    return Opacity(
      opacity: settled * opacity,
      child: Transform.translate(
        offset: offset + Offset(0, (1 - settled) * 28),
        child: Transform.rotate(
          angle: angle,
          child: Transform.scale(
            scale: scale * (0.82 + 0.18 * settled),
            child: child,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// The cell of a page: its [symbol], or its [icon] on the primary colour,
/// with [number] in its corner, as an element has one.
class _Cell extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Cell({required this.number, this.symbol, this.icon});

  final int number;
  final String? symbol;
  final IconData? icon;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final colors = theme.colorScheme;
    final filled = symbol == null;
    final ink = filled ? colors.onPrimary : colors.onSurface;
    // The letters and the number are part of the picture, in a cell of a
    // fixed size: they do not grow with the text size of the device.
    return MediaQuery.withNoTextScaling(
      child: Container(
        width: 164,
        height: 164,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: filled ? colors.primary : colors.surfaceContainerLowest,
          border: Border.all(color: filled ? colors.primary : ink, width: 2),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.10),
              blurRadius: 40,
              offset: const Offset(0, 20),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Stack(
          children: [
            Positioned(
              top: 10,
              right: 14,
              child: Text(
                '$number',
                // The monospaced font of the device: Android has it under
                // the first name, and iOS under the next.
                style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                  fontFamily: 'monospace',
                  fontFamilyFallback: const ['Menlo', 'Courier'],
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  color: ink,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Center(
              child: symbol == null
                  ? Icon(icon, size: 84, color: ink)
                  : Text(
                      symbol!,
                      style: theme.textTheme.displayLarge?.copyWith(
                        fontSize: 80,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                        letterSpacing: -3,
                        color: ink,
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A grid of square cells in [color], moved sideways by [shift].
class _GridPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _GridPainter({required this.color, required this.shift});

  final Color color;
  final double shift;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    const cell = 52.0;
    final line = Paint()
      ..color = color
      ..strokeWidth = 1;
    // The grid is centred, so that the cell of the page sits on its lines.
    final left = (size.width / 2 + shift) % cell;
    for (var x = left; x < size.width; x += cell) {
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(x, 0), Offset(x, size.height), line);
    }
    final top = (size.height / 2) % cell;
    for (var y = top; y < size.height; y += cell) {
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(0, y), Offset(size.width, y), line);
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_GridPainter oldDelegate) =>
      oldDelegate.color != color || oldDelegate.shift != shift;
}
=== lib/features/onboarding/onboarding_screen.dart (onboarding) ===
import 'dart:ui' show lerpDouble;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:my_app/core/l10n/l10n.dart';

import '../../core/onboarding/onboarding_steps.dart';
import 'onboarding_pages.dart';
import 'onboarding_status.dart';

/// The onboarding of the app, which the app shows on its first launch in
/// place of every other screen: the pages of [onboardingPages], with the
/// steps that the modules of the app add, [appOnboardingSteps], before the
/// last of them. Skip is above the pages, and below them are a mark for
/// each page and a button that leads to the next page and, on the last
/// one, into the app.
///
/// A step is a page in the look of the others, with the icon of the step
/// in its cell. On the page of a step, the button below the pages is the
/// action of the step: it calls the function of the step, spins until the
/// future of that function completes, or takes no tap until then in an app
/// that asks for less motion, and then shows the next page, also when the
/// future fails. A step may have a second button below it, which
/// goes on without the action. Skip skips the steps too.
///
/// Skip and the last button finish the onboarding. The screen does not
/// navigate, and nothing navigates to it: the router shows it while the
/// onboarding is not finished, and then the screen that the app starts on.
/// The next launches of the app start there.
///
/// A navigation to the route of the screen once the onboarding is
/// finished, such as a link, shows the screen that the app starts on too,
/// or the target of another guard of the app while that one does not
/// allow. Only [OnboardingStatus.restart] shows the onboarding again.
class OnboardingScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  /// Creates the screen.
  const OnboardingScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<OnboardingScreen> createState() => _OnboardingScreenState();
}

class _OnboardingScreenState extends State<OnboardingScreen> {
  final _controller = PageController();

  /// The steps that the screen shows: those of [appOnboardingSteps] whose
  /// showIf keeps them when the screen opens. A step stays while the
  /// screen is shown, also once its showIf would leave it out.
  late final List<OnboardingStep> _steps = [
    for (final step in appOnboardingSteps)
      if (step.showIf?.call() ?? true) step,
  ];

  /// The index of the page that the user sees.
  var _page = 0;

  /// Whether the action of a step runs: until its future completes, the
  /// pages stay where they are and the buttons of the step take no tap.
  var _running = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  /// Shows the page after the one that the user sees: at once in an app
  /// that asks for less motion.
  void _next() {
    if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      _controller.jumpToPage(_page + 1);
    } else {
      _controller.nextPage(
        duration: Durations.long2,
        curve: Easing.emphasizedDecelerate,
      );
    }
  }

  /// Calls the action of [step] with the context of the screen, and shows
  /// the next page once its future completes, also when it fails: the error
  /// goes on to the handlers of the errors of the app that nothing caught.
  Future<void> _run(OnboardingStep step) async {
    setState(() => _running = true);
    try {
      await step.run(context);
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _running = false);
        _next();
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final own = onboardingPages(context);
    // The steps come before the last page of the list.
    final first = own.isEmpty ? 0 : own.length - 1;
    final pages = [
      ...own.take(first),
      for (final step in _steps)
        OnboardingPage(
          icon: step.icon,
          title: step.title(context),
          text: step.text(context),
        ),
      ...own.skip(first),
    ];
    final isLast = _page == pages.length - 1;
    // The step whose page the user sees, if it is one.
    final step = _page >= first && _page < first + _steps.length
        ? _steps[_page - first]
        : null;
    // While the action of a step runs, its button spins, but not in an app
    // that asks for less motion, where it takes no tap instead.
    final spins = _running && !MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context);
    // What the button below the pages does, and what it says.
    final VoidCallback? onPressed;
    final String label;
    if (step == null) {
      onPressed = isLast ? onboardingStatus.complete : _next;
      label = isLast
          ? context.l10n.onboardingDone
          : context.l10n.onboardingNext;
    } else {
      // A button that spins keeps its colour and does nothing.
      onPressed = _running ? (spins ? () {} : null) : () => _run(step);
      label = step.action(context);
    }
    // The steps with a second button, which is below the button of the
    // pages while the page of its step is shown.
    final withLater = [
      for (final other in _steps)
        if (other.later != null) other,
    ];
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final colors = theme.colorScheme;
    // How long Skip and the label of the button take to change with the
    // page: no time in an app that asks for less motion.
    final fade = MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
        ? Duration.zero
        : Durations.short4;
    // The colour that the background has reached on the last page.
    final end = Color.alphaBlend(
      colors.secondaryContainer.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
      colors.surface,
    );
    return Scaffold(
      body: AnimatedBuilder(
        animation: _controller,
        builder: (context, child) => ColoredBox(
          color: Color.lerp(
            colors.surface,
            end,
            pages.length < 2
                ? 0
                : (OnboardingPageScope.turnedOf(_controller) /
                          (pages.length - 1))
                      .clamp(0, 1),
          )!,
          child: child,
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Column(
            children: [
              ConstrainedBox(
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 48),
                child: Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 12),
                    // Skip fades away on the last page, where a tap no
                    // longer reaches it, and is gone once it has faded.
                    child: IgnorePointer(
                      ignoring: isLast,
                      child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                        duration: fade,
                        child: isLast
                            ? const SizedBox.shrink()
                            : TextButton(
                                onPressed: onboardingStatus.complete,
                                child: OnboardingWords(
                                  context.l10n.onboardingSkip,
                                ),
                              ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                // The pages stay where they are while the action of a step
                // runs.
                child: IgnorePointer(
                  ignoring: _running,
                  child: PageView(
                    controller: _controller,
                    onPageChanged: (page) {
                      HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
                      setState(() => _page = page);
                    },
                    children: [
                      for (final (index, page) in pages.indexed)
                        OnboardingPageScope(
                          index: index,
                          controller: _controller,
                          child: page,
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(28, 20, 28, 16),
                // The labels of the buttons grow only by half with the text
                // size of the device: the pages take the room that the
                // buttons leave, and would have none left on a small phone.
                child: MediaQuery.withClampedTextScaling(
                  maxScaleFactor: 1.5,
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      Align(
                        alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                        child: _PageMarks(
                          controller: _controller,
                          count: pages.length,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      FilledButton(
                        onPressed: onPressed,
                        child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                          duration: fade,
                          child: _ButtonLabel(
                            label,
                            spins: spins,
                            key: ValueKey<Object>(step ?? isLast),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      // The second button of a step, in a place of the height
                      // of the tallest of them, so that the pages keep their
                      // height from page to page. Only the one of the step
                      // whose page is shown is on the screen: the others take
                      // no tap and no focus.
                      if (withLater.isNotEmpty)
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
                          child: IndexedStack(
                            alignment: Alignment.center,
                            index: step?.later == null
                                ? withLater.length
                                : withLater.indexOf(step!),
                            children: [
                              for (final other in withLater)
                                TextButton(
                                  onPressed: _running ? null : _next,
                                  child: OnboardingWords(other.later!(context)),
                                ),
                              const SizedBox.shrink(),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// The label of the button below the pages, or what spins in its place
/// while the action of a step runs.
class _ButtonLabel extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ButtonLabel(this.label, {required this.spins, super.key});

  final String label;
  final bool spins;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Stack(
    alignment: Alignment.center,
    children: [
      // The label keeps its place, so that the button keeps its size.
      Visibility.maintain(visible: !spins, child: OnboardingWords(label)),
      if (spins)
        // In the colour that the button gives its label.
        SizedBox.square(
          dimension: 20,
          child: CircularProgressIndicator(
            strokeWidth: 2.5,
            color: DefaultTextStyle.of(context).style.color,
          ),
        ),
    ],
  );
}

/// A mark for each of [count] pages, with the one of the page that the user
/// sees stretched and in the primary colour. The marks follow the pages
/// while they turn.
class _PageMarks extends StatelessWidget {
  const _PageMarks({required this.controller, required this.count});

  final PageController controller;
  final int count;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return ExcludeSemantics(
      child: AnimatedBuilder(
        animation: controller,
        builder: (context, _) {
          final turned = OnboardingPageScope.turnedOf(controller);
          return Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              for (var index = 0; index < count; index++)
                _mark(
                  colors,
                  // How much the mark is that of the page on the screen.
                  (1 - (turned - index).abs()).clamp(0, 1),
                ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  /// A mark that is [current] of the way from that of another page to that
  /// of the page on the screen.
  Widget _mark(ColorScheme colors, double current) => Container(
    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6),
    height: 8,
    width: lerpDouble(8, 28, current),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(
      color: Color.lerp(
        colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
        colors.primary,
        current,
      ),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
    ),
  );
}
=== lib/features/onboarding/onboarding_status.dart (onboarding) ===
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../core/preferences/app_preferences.dart';

/// Whether the user has finished the onboarding of the app, which the app
/// remembers between its launches.
///
/// Until the user has, the router shows the onboarding in place of every
/// other screen: the guard of the routes of the onboarding asks
/// [onboardingCompleted]. So the app shows the onboarding exactly while it
/// is not finished, and nothing navigates to it: [OnboardingStatus.restart]
/// shows it again.
final onboardingStatus = OnboardingStatus._();

/// Keeps whether the user has finished the onboarding, tells its listeners
/// when that changes, and saves it in the preferences of the app. The app
/// has one, [onboardingStatus].
final class OnboardingStatus {
  OnboardingStatus._();

  /// The key in the preferences under which it is saved.
  static const _key = 'onboarding.completed';

  /// The preferences that remember it, once the app opened them.
  AppPreferences? _preferences;

  final ValueNotifier<bool> _completed = ValueNotifier(false);

  /// Whether the user has finished the onboarding. It notifies its
  /// listeners when that changes.
  ValueListenable<bool> get completed => _completed;

  /// Finishes the onboarding, at once, and saves that: the future completes
  /// once it is saved. Until the app opened its preferences for the first
  /// time, it changes only memory.
  ///
  /// The router leaves the onboarding as soon as this is called, so call it
  /// outside the build of a frame, such as in the handler of a tap.
  ///
  /// If the preferences fail to save it, the future completes with their
  /// error. The app has left the onboarding by then, and its next launch
  /// shows the onboarding again.
  Future<void> complete() async {
    _completed.value = true;
    await _preferences?.setBool(_key, true);
  }

  /// Starts the onboarding again, at once, and saves that: the future
  /// completes once it is saved. Call it where the user asks to see the
  /// onboarding again, such as in the handler of a tap on a row of the
  /// settings, and navigate nowhere.
  ///
  /// The router shows the onboarding as soon as this is called, in place of
  /// the screen that the user is on. Once the onboarding is finished again,
  /// the router shows that screen again. If the user was on a page pushed
  /// over another screen, it shows the screen below the pushed pages
  /// instead. An app that is closed before that shows the onboarding on its
  /// next launch.
  ///
  /// Call it outside the build of a frame, where the router cannot
  /// navigate. In a build, Flutter reports an error, and the app shows the
  /// onboarding only at its next navigation.
  ///
  /// Call it after `bootstrap()`. Until the app opened its preferences for
  /// the first time, it changes only memory, and the start-up then takes
  /// what the preferences have saved.
  ///
  /// If the preferences fail to save it, the future completes with their
  /// error, and the next launch of the app finds the onboarding finished.
  Future<void> restart() async {
    _completed.value = false;
    await _preferences?.setBool(_key, false);
  }

  /// Takes what [preferences] have saved, and keeps the current value when
  /// they have nothing saved. It keeps [preferences] for [complete] and
  /// [restart].
  void _restore(AppPreferences preferences) {
    _preferences = preferences;
    _completed.value = preferences.getBool(_key) ?? _completed.value;
  }
}

/// Whether the user has finished the onboarding, as the guard of the routes
/// of the onboarding asks it: while the value is `false`, the router shows
/// the onboarding in place of every other screen.
ValueListenable<bool> onboardingCompleted() => onboardingStatus.completed;

/// Restores from [preferences], which the app opened, whether the user has
/// finished the onboarding, and keeps them for when that changes: a
/// restorer of the preferences, which the app calls before its first frame.
void restoreOnboarding(AppPreferences preferences) =>
    onboardingStatus._restore(preferences);

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'paywall_widgets.dart';

/// The name of the app, as the card shows it.
const _appName = 'My App';

/// The symbol of the app in its cell, as an element of the periodic table
/// has one: the first letters of the first two words of its name, or the
/// first two letters of a name of one word.
const _appSymbol = 'Ma';

/// The number in the cell of the app: how many letters and digits its name
/// has.
const _appNumber = 5;

/// The colours of Say My Frame, which the card keeps in the light and in
/// the dark theme, as the start screen of SMF does.
const _brandGreen = Color(0xFF0F3326);
const _brandCream = Color(0xFFF1F1E8);
const _brandAccent = Color(0xFF4ADE80);

/// The side of the cell of the app in the card, the space between the cell
/// and the edges of the card, the side of a cell of the table on the right
/// of the card, the space between two of them, and their space from the
/// edges of the card.
const _cellSide = 76.0;
const _cardPadding = 22.0;
const _tableCell = 40.0;
const _tableGap = 8.0;
const _tableInset = 20.0;

/// The card in the colours of Say My Frame at the top of the paywall and of
/// the screen of the subscription: the app as an element of a table, its
/// name with `Premium` above it, and on the right the cells of a table that
/// light up one after another while [animation] runs and end in a cell in
/// the accent with the icon of premium access, which grows a little once:

/// With [badge], such as `Unlocked`, it shows the badge next to `Premium`.

    required this.premium,
    this.badge,
    super.key,
  });

  final Animation<double> animation;

  /// The text `Premium` in the language of the app.

  /// The text of the badge next to `Premium`, or `null`.
  final String? badge;

  @override

      decoration: BoxDecoration(

      ),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(28),
        child: Stack(
          children: [
            Positioned.fill(
              child: ExcludeSemantics(
                child: CustomPaint(painter: _TablePainter(animation)),
              ),
            ),

              top: _tableInset,
              end: _tableInset,

                    header: true,

                    excludeSemantics: true,

                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Wrap(
                          spacing: 10,
                          runSpacing: 6,
                          crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,

                            // A label, which grows only by half with the
                            // text size of the device.
                            MediaQuery.withClampedTextScaling(
                              maxScaleFactor: 1.5,
                              child: Text(
                                premium.toUpperCase(),
                                style: monospace.copyWith(
                                  fontSize: 13,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                  letterSpacing: 2,
                                  color: _brandAccent,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            if (badge != null) _Badge(badge),
                          ],
                        ),

                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                          maxScaleFactor: 1.5,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// The badge of a [PremiumCard], such as `Unlocked`: the text in the
/// accent of Say My Frame in a pill of that accent.
class _Badge extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Badge(this.text);

  final String text;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => DecoratedBox(
    decoration: BoxDecoration(

/// The cell at the top of the table of a [PremiumCard]: it fills with the
/// accent of Say My Frame once the other cells lit up, with the icon of
/// premium access, which grows a little and back once.
class _UnlockCell extends StatelessWidget {
  const _UnlockCell({required this.animation});

  final Animation<double> animation;

  /// When the cell fills, and when it grows and comes back, in the
  /// entrance of the card.
  static const _fill = Interval(0.6, 0.78, curve: Curves.easeOut);
  static final _grow = TweenSequence<double>([
    TweenSequenceItem(
      tween: Tween<double>(
        begin: 1,
        end: 1.06,
      ).chain(CurveTween(curve: Curves.easeOut)),
      weight: 1,
    ),
    TweenSequenceItem(
      tween: Tween<double>(
        begin: 1.06,
        end: 1,
      ).chain(CurveTween(curve: Curves.easeIn)),
      weight: 1,
    ),
  ]).chain(CurveTween(curve: const Interval(0.74, 1)));

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => ExcludeSemantics(
    child: AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: animation,
      builder: (context, _) {
        final filled = _fill.transform(animation.value);
        return Transform.scale(
          scale: _grow.transform(animation.value),
          child: Container(
            width: _tableCell,
            height: _tableCell,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: _brandAccent.withValues(alpha: filled),
              border: Border.all(
                color: _brandCream.withValues(alpha: 0.14 + 0.86 * filled),
              ),
            ),
            child: Opacity(
              opacity: filled,
              child: const Icon(
                Icons.workspace_premium_rounded,
                size: 24,
                color: _brandGreen,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    ),
  );
}

/// The cells of a table on the right of a [PremiumCard], but for the cell
/// at its top right, which [_UnlockCell] is: outlines, some of which light
/// up one after another while [animation] runs.
class _TablePainter extends CustomPainter {
  _TablePainter(this.animation) : super(repaint: animation);

  final Animation<double> animation;

  /// The cells that light up, by column from the right and row from the
  /// top, each with its colour and the order in which it lights up.
  static const _lit = <(int, int, Color, int)>[
    (2, 1, Color(0x33F1F1E8), 0),
    (1, 2, Color(0x664ADE80), 1),
    (3, 0, Color(0x1FF1F1E8), 2),
    (1, 1, Color(0x994ADE80), 3),
    (0, 1, Color(0x33F1F1E8), 4),
  ];

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final outline = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 1;
    for (var column = 0; column < 4; column++) {
      for (var row = 0; row < 3; row++) {
        // The cell at the top right is the cell that unlocks.
        if (column == 0 && row == 0) continue;
        final rect = Rect.fromLTWH(
          size.width -
              _tableInset -
              _tableCell -
              column * (_tableCell + _tableGap),
          _tableInset + row * (_tableCell + _tableGap),
          _tableCell,
          _tableCell,
        );
        // A narrow card has no room for every column: it leaves out those
        // that would reach the cell of the app on its left.
        if (rect.left < _cardPadding + _cellSide + _tableGap) continue;
        // The outlines fade towards the cell of the app.
        outline.color = _brandCream.withValues(alpha: 0.14 - column * 0.03);
        canvas.drawRect(rect, outline);
        for (final (litColumn, litRow, color, order) in _lit) {
          if (litColumn != column || litRow != row) continue;
          final start = 0.15 + order * 0.08;
          final shown = Interval(
            start,
            (start + 0.25).clamp(0, 1),
            curve: Curves.easeOut,
          ).transform(animation.value);
          canvas.drawRect(
            rect,
            Paint()..color = color.withValues(alpha: color.a * shown),
          );
        }
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_TablePainter oldDelegate) =>
      oldDelegate.animation != animation;
}

=== lib/features/paywall/paywall_cubit.dart (paywall (bloc)) ===
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import '../../core/purchases/app_purchases.dart';
import 'paywall_state.dart';

/// The state of the paywall: it loads the plans of the current offer, buys
/// the selected plan and restores purchases through the purchases of the
/// app, and tells which call is on its way and what the last one ended
/// with.
class PaywallCubit extends Cubit<PaywallState> {
  /// Creates the cubit that buys through [_purchases] and opens a link with
  /// [_openLink], and loads the plans.
  PaywallCubit(this._purchases, this._openLink) : super(const PaywallState()) {
    _purchases.hasPremium.addListener(_follow);
    unawaited(load());
  }

  final AppPurchasesController _purchases;
  final Future<void> Function(Uri url) _openLink;

  /// Whether a purchase or a restore of this cubit is on its way.
  var _calling = false;

  /// Loads the plans of the current offer, and selects the plan that the
  /// paywall selects first.
  Future<void> load() async {
    if (state.busy != null) return;
    emit(const PaywallState());
    try {
      final plans = await _purchases.plans();
      if (!isClosed) {
        emit(PaywallState(plans: plans, selected: preselectedPlan(plans)));
      }
    } on PurchasesFailure catch (failure) {
      if (!isClosed) emit(PaywallState(loadFailure: failure));
    }
  }

  /// Selects [plan], unless a call is on its way.
  void select(Plan plan) {
    if (state.busy != null) return;
    emit(state.selecting(plan));
  }

  /// Buys the selected plan.
  ///
  /// Once the user has premium access, the router closes the paywall, so
  /// the state stays busy for as long as the user has it. When the user
  /// cancels, the state is again as it was, and a payment that waits for an
  /// approval tells the user so.
  Future<void> purchase() async {
    final plan = state.selected;
    if (plan == null) return;
    await _run(PaywallAction.purchase, () => _purchases.purchase(plan));
  }

  /// Restores what the store account of the user bought.
  ///
  /// Once the user has premium access, the router closes the paywall, so
  /// the state stays busy for as long as the user has it. A restore that
  /// finds nothing tells the user so.
  Future<void> restore() => _run(
    PaywallAction.restore,
    () async => await _purchases.restore() is PremiumAccess,
    otherwise: PaywallNotice.nothingToRestore,
  );

  /// Opens [url], a link of the paywall.
  Future<void> openLink(Uri url) => _openLink(url);

  /// Makes [call], the call of [action], unless a call is on its way or the
  /// plans load. [call] returns whether the user has premium access then,
  /// and the state stays busy if so. Otherwise it is idle again, with the
  /// notice [otherwise], if any.
  Future<void> _run(
    PaywallAction action,
    Future<bool> Function() call, {
    PaywallNotice? otherwise,
  }) async {
    if (state.busy != null || state.loading) return;
    _calling = true;
    emit(state.busyWith(action));
    final bool premium;
    try {
      premium = await call();
    } on PurchasesFailure catch (failure) {
      _calling = false;
      // The user may have left the paywall while the call was on its way.
      if (isClosed) return;
      emit(
        failure.reason == PurchasesFailureReason.pending
            ? state.idle(notice: PaywallNotice.pending)
            : state.idle(failure: failure),
      );
      return;
    }
    _calling = false;
    if (isClosed) return;
    if (premium) {
      _follow();
    } else {
      emit(state.idle(notice: otherwise));
    }
  }

  /// Ends the busy state that a call left once the user has no premium
  /// access: the access ended again before the router took the paywall
  /// away, and the paywall is back.
  void _follow() {
    if (_calling || isClosed || state.busy == null) return;
    if (!_purchases.hasPremium.value) emit(state.idle());
  }

  @override
  Future<void> close() {
    _purchases.hasPremium.removeListener(_follow);
    return super.close();
  }
}

/// is `true`, the router closes the paywall and shows the route.

=== lib/features/paywall/paywall_links.dart (paywall) ===
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:my_app/core/env/app_env.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

/// The name of the key of the address of the terms of use in `.env`.
const String paywallTermsKey = 'PAYWALL_TERMS_URL';

/// The name of the key of the address of the privacy policy in `.env`.
const String paywallPrivacyKey = 'PAYWALL_PRIVACY_URL';

/// The standard license agreement of Apple, which the terms of use of the
/// paywall lead to on iOS while `.env` has no address of the terms of use.
final Uri appleStandardEula = Uri.parse(
  'https://www.apple.com/legal/internet-services/itunes/dev/stdeula/',
);

/// The address of the terms of use that the paywall links to: the value of
/// [paywallTermsKey] in `.env`, or on iOS and macOS the standard license
/// agreement of Apple while that value is empty or no web address, and
/// otherwise `null`, for no link.
Uri? paywallTermsUrl() =>
    webAddressOf(AppEnv.paywallTermsUrl) ??
    switch (defaultTargetPlatform) {
      TargetPlatform.iOS || TargetPlatform.macOS => appleStandardEula,
      _ => null,
    };

/// The address of the privacy policy that the paywall links to: the value
/// of [paywallPrivacyKey] in `.env`, or `null`, for no link, while that
/// value is empty or no web address.
Uri? paywallPrivacyUrl() => webAddressOf(AppEnv.paywallPrivacyUrl);

/// The keys of `.env` whose values the paywall cannot link to, since they
/// are empty or no web address: in a debug build, the paywall names them
/// for the developer of the app.
List<String> missingPaywallKeys() => [
  if (webAddressOf(AppEnv.paywallTermsUrl) == null) paywallTermsKey,
  if (webAddressOf(AppEnv.paywallPrivacyUrl) == null) paywallPrivacyKey,
];

/// [value] as a web address, with `https` or `http` and a host, or `null`
/// for an empty value and for anything else.
Uri? webAddressOf(String? value) {
  final address = Uri.tryParse(value?.trim() ?? '');
  if (address == null || address.host.isEmpty) return null;
  return address.isScheme('https') || address.isScheme('http') ? address : null;
}

/// Opens [url] outside the app: a page in the browser, or a page of the
/// store in the app of the store.
Future<void> openPaywallLink(Uri url) async {
  await launchUrl(url, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
}

/// The paywall: the [PaywallView] with the state of a [PaywallCubit].

  const PaywallScreen({super.key});

  @override

        onRestore: context.read<PaywallCubit>().restore,
        onRetry: context.read<PaywallCubit>().load,

  );

=== lib/features/paywall/paywall_state.dart (paywall) ===
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../core/purchases/app_purchases.dart';

/// A call of the paywall that is on its way.
enum PaywallAction {
  /// Buying the selected plan.
  purchase,

  /// Restoring what the store account of the user bought.
  restore,
}

/// What the paywall tells the user after a call that did not fail.
enum PaywallNotice {
  /// The payment waits for an approval, as one that a parent approves: the
  /// access comes once the store took the payment, and the router closes
  /// the paywall then.
  pending,

  /// A restore found nothing that the store account bought.
  nothingToRestore,
}

/// The state of the paywall: the plans of the current offer, or why they
/// did not load, the plan that the user selected, which call is on its way,
/// and what the last call ended with.
@immutable
final class PaywallState {
  /// Creates the state, which is loading the plans without [plans] and
  /// [loadFailure].
  const PaywallState({
    this.plans,
    this.loadFailure,
    this.selected,
    this.busy,
    this.failure,
    this.notice,
  });

  /// The plans of the current offer, in the order of the provider of
  /// purchases, or `null` while they load or when they did not.
  final List<Plan>? plans;

  /// Why the plans did not load, or `null`.
  final PurchasesFailure? loadFailure;

  /// The plan that the button of the paywall buys, or `null` while there
  /// are no plans.
  final Plan? selected;

  /// The call that is on its way, or `null` when none is. While it is, the
  /// paywall takes no other call.
  ///
  /// It stays so after a purchase or a restore that gave the user premium
  /// access, for as long as the user has it: what comes next is up to the
  /// router, which closes the paywall. The paywall neither closes itself
  /// nor navigates. When the access ends before the router took the
  /// paywall away, the paywall is idle again.
  ///
  /// While the plans load, the paywall takes no call, so that no plan comes
  /// in to be bought while a restore is on its way.
  final PaywallAction? busy;

  /// Why the last call failed, or `null` if it did not.
  final PurchasesFailure? failure;

  /// What the last call that did not fail tells the user, or `null`.
  final PaywallNotice? notice;

  /// Whether the plans are loading.
  bool get loading => plans == null && loadFailure == null;

  /// The state with [plan] selected, with nothing on its way and nothing to
  /// tell.
  PaywallState selecting(Plan plan) =>
      PaywallState(plans: plans, loadFailure: loadFailure, selected: plan);

  /// The state while [action] is on its way. The plans, or why they did not
  /// load, stay as they are.
  PaywallState busyWith(PaywallAction action) => PaywallState(
    plans: plans,
    loadFailure: loadFailure,
    selected: selected,
    busy: action,
  );

  /// The state with nothing on its way, which tells of [failure] or
  /// [notice], if any. The plans, or why they did not load, stay as they
  /// are.
  PaywallState idle({PurchasesFailure? failure, PaywallNotice? notice}) =>
      PaywallState(
        plans: plans,
        loadFailure: loadFailure,
        selected: selected,
        failure: failure,
        notice: notice,
      );

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      other is PaywallState &&
      other.plans == plans &&
      other.loadFailure == loadFailure &&
      other.selected == selected &&
      other.busy == busy &&
      other.failure == failure &&
      other.notice == notice;

  @override
  int get hashCode =>
      Object.hash(plans, loadFailure, selected, busy, failure, notice);
}

/// How often a plan is billed, for the periods that the paywall has words
/// for: those that the stores offer.
enum PlanTerm {
  /// Every week.
  week,

  /// Every month.
  month,

  /// Every 2 months.
  twoMonths,

  /// Every 3 months.
  threeMonths,

  /// Every 6 months.
  sixMonths,

  /// Every year.
  year,
}

/// The term of [period], or `null` for a period that the paywall has no
/// words for. Seven days are a week, and twelve months a year.
PlanTerm? termOf(BillingPeriod period) => switch ((period.unit, period.count)) {
  (BillingUnit.day, 7) || (BillingUnit.week, 1) => PlanTerm.week,
  (BillingUnit.month, 1) => PlanTerm.month,
  (BillingUnit.month, 2) => PlanTerm.twoMonths,
  (BillingUnit.month, 3) => PlanTerm.threeMonths,
  (BillingUnit.month, 6) => PlanTerm.sixMonths,
  (BillingUnit.month, 12) || (BillingUnit.year, 1) => PlanTerm.year,
  _ => null,
};

/// How long an intro offer lasts, for the lengths that the paywall has
/// words for: those of the free trials of the stores.
enum OfferLength {
  /// 3 days.
  threeDays,

  /// 1 week.
  oneWeek,

  /// 2 weeks.
  twoWeeks,

  /// 1 month.
  oneMonth,

  /// 2 months.
  twoMonths,

  /// 3 months.
  threeMonths,

  /// 6 months.
  sixMonths,

  /// 1 year.
  oneYear,
}

/// How long [offer] lasts in all, its period times its cycles, or `null`
/// for a length that the paywall has no words for. Seven days are a week,
/// fourteen days two weeks, and twelve months a year.
OfferLength? lengthOf(IntroOffer offer) =>
    switch ((offer.period.unit, offer.period.count * offer.cycles)) {
      (BillingUnit.day, 3) => OfferLength.threeDays,
      (BillingUnit.day, 7) || (BillingUnit.week, 1) => OfferLength.oneWeek,
      (BillingUnit.day, 14) || (BillingUnit.week, 2) => OfferLength.twoWeeks,
      (BillingUnit.month, 1) => OfferLength.oneMonth,
      (BillingUnit.month, 2) => OfferLength.twoMonths,
      (BillingUnit.month, 3) => OfferLength.threeMonths,
      (BillingUnit.month, 6) => OfferLength.sixMonths,
      (BillingUnit.month, 12) || (BillingUnit.year, 1) => OfferLength.oneYear,
      _ => null,
    };

/// The plan of [plans] that the paywall selects when it shows them: the
/// first that renews every year, or else the first plan.
Plan? preselectedPlan(List<Plan> plans) {
  for (final plan in plans) {
    if (_yearly(plan)) return plan;
  }
  return plans.isEmpty ? null : plans.first;
}

/// Whether [plan] is the best value of [plans]: a plan that renews every
/// year and costs less than twelve times a plan of [plans] that renews
/// every month, in the same currency.
///
/// The paywall says so on the plan, and never shows a yearly plan by what
/// it costs a month, which Google Play does not allow.
bool isBestValue(Plan plan, List<Plan> plans) {
  if (!_yearly(plan)) return false;
  for (final other in plans) {
    final monthly = switch (other.period) {
      final period? => termOf(period) == PlanTerm.month,
      null => false,
    };
    if (monthly &&
        other.currencyCode == plan.currencyCode &&
        plan.amount < other.amount * 12) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/// Whether [plan] renews every year.
bool _yearly(Plan plan) => switch (plan.period) {
  final period? => termOf(period) == PlanTerm.year,
  null => false,
};
=== lib/features/paywall/paywall_texts.dart (paywall) ===
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:my_app/core/l10n/l10n.dart';

import '../../core/purchases/app_purchases.dart';
import 'paywall_state.dart';

// The texts of the paywall that depend on a plan. A text of the app has no
// parameters, so these put the texts of closed sets together with the
// prices that the store gives: the name and the suffix of the price for
// each term of a plan, and the length of each intro offer that the stores
// have. A term or a length outside those sets gets a text without it.

/// The name of [plan] on the paywall: the name of its term, `Lifetime` for
/// a plan paid once, or the title of its product in the store for a term
/// that the paywall has no name for.
String planNameOf(BuildContext context, Plan plan) => switch (plan.period) {
  null => context.l10n.paywallPlanLifetime,
  final period => termNameOf(context, period) ?? plan.title,
};

/// The name of the plan of [period], such as `Monthly`, or `null` for a
/// period that the paywall has no name for.
String? termNameOf(BuildContext context, BillingPeriod period) =>
    switch (termOf(period)) {
      PlanTerm.week => context.l10n.paywallPlanWeekly,
      PlanTerm.month => context.l10n.paywallPlanMonthly,
      PlanTerm.twoMonths => context.l10n.paywallPlanTwoMonths,
      PlanTerm.threeMonths => context.l10n.paywallPlanThreeMonths,
      PlanTerm.sixMonths => context.l10n.paywallPlanSixMonths,
      PlanTerm.year => context.l10n.paywallPlanYearly,
      null => null,
    };

/// What follows the price of a plan of [period], such as `/ month`, or
/// `once` for a plan paid once, or `null` for a period that the paywall has
/// no words for.
String? priceSuffixOf(BuildContext context, BillingPeriod? period) =>
    switch (period) {
      null => context.l10n.paywallOnce,
      final period => switch (termOf(period)) {
        PlanTerm.week => context.l10n.paywallPerWeek,
        PlanTerm.month => context.l10n.paywallPerMonth,
        PlanTerm.twoMonths => context.l10n.paywallPerTwoMonths,
        PlanTerm.threeMonths => context.l10n.paywallPerThreeMonths,
        PlanTerm.sixMonths => context.l10n.paywallPerSixMonths,
        PlanTerm.year => context.l10n.paywallPerYear,
        null => null,
      },
    };

/// The price of [plan] with what follows it, such as `$4.99 / month`, with
/// no-break spaces, so that a line never breaks inside it.
String priceOf(BuildContext context, Plan plan) => [
  plan.price,
  ?priceSuffixOf(context, plan.period),
].join(' ').replaceAll(' ', _noBreakSpace);

/// The no-break space, which keeps the words on each side of it on one line.
const String _noBreakSpace = '\u00A0';

/// How long an intro offer of [length] lasts, such as `1 week`.
String lengthTextOf(BuildContext context, OfferLength length) =>
    switch (length) {
      OfferLength.threeDays => context.l10n.paywallThreeDays,
      OfferLength.oneWeek => context.l10n.paywallOneWeek,
      OfferLength.twoWeeks => context.l10n.paywallTwoWeeks,
      OfferLength.oneMonth => context.l10n.paywallOneMonth,
      OfferLength.twoMonths => context.l10n.paywallTwoMonths,
      OfferLength.threeMonths => context.l10n.paywallThreeMonths,
      OfferLength.sixMonths => context.l10n.paywallSixMonths,
      OfferLength.oneYear => context.l10n.paywallOneYear,
    };

/// What [offer] gives, as the card of its plan shows it: `1 week free` for
/// a free trial, `$1.99 for 3 months` for a lower price, or with the
/// suffix of its period, `$0.99 / month for 3 months`, for a lower price of
/// several periods. For a length that the paywall has no words for, it is
/// `Free trial` or `Introductory price`.
String offerTextOf(BuildContext context, IntroOffer offer) {
  final length = switch (lengthOf(offer)) {
    final length? => lengthTextOf(context, length),
    null => null,
  };
  if (offer.isFree) {
    final free = context.l10n.paywallFree;
    return length == null ? context.l10n.paywallFreeTrial : '$length $free';
  }
  final suffix = offer.cycles > 1 ? priceSuffixOf(context, offer.period) : null;
  if (length == null || (offer.cycles > 1 && suffix == null)) {
    return context.l10n.paywallIntroPrice;
  }
  return [
    offer.price,
    ?suffix,
    context.l10n.paywallForLength,
    length,
  ].join(' ');
}

/// What [plan] costs after its intro offer, as the paywall says right
/// below its button, such as `1 week free, then $29.99 / year`, or `null`
/// for a plan without an intro offer.
String? afterOfferTextOf(BuildContext context, Plan plan) {
  final offer = plan.introOffer;
  if (offer == null) return null;
  final then = context.l10n.paywallThen;
  return '${offerTextOf(context, offer)}, $then ${priceOf(context, plan)}';
}

/// The label of the button that buys [plan]: `Start free trial` for a plan
/// with a free trial, `Buy` for a plan paid once, and `Subscribe` for any
/// other plan and while there is none.
String purchaseLabelOf(BuildContext context, Plan? plan) => switch (plan) {
  Plan(introOffer: IntroOffer(isFree: true)) => context.l10n.paywallStartTrial,
  Plan(period: null) => context.l10n.paywallBuy,
  _ => context.l10n.paywallSubscribe,
};

/// What the user agrees to by buying [plan], below the button: that a
/// plan paid once is no subscription, or that the plan renews until the
/// user cancels it in the store of the platform, with what the user pays
/// after an intro offer.
String termsTextOf(BuildContext context, Plan plan) {
  if (plan.period == null) return context.l10n.paywallTermsLifetime;
  final store = switch (defaultTargetPlatform) {
    TargetPlatform.iOS ||
    TargetPlatform.macOS => context.l10n.paywallTermsApple,
    _ => context.l10n.paywallTermsGoogle,
  };
  final offer = switch (plan.introOffer) {
    null => null,
    IntroOffer(isFree: true) => context.l10n.paywallTermsTrial,
    IntroOffer() => context.l10n.paywallTermsIntro,
  };
  return [store, ?offer].join(' ');
}

/// The text of the app for [reason], the reason of a failure of purchases.
///
/// It has a text for every reason: a reason that the app gets later has
/// none here, and the app does not compile until it has.
String failureTextOf(
  BuildContext context,
  PurchasesFailureReason reason,
) => switch (reason) {
  PurchasesFailureReason.network => context.l10n.paywallFailureNetwork,
  PurchasesFailureReason.pending => context.l10n.paywallPendingNotice,
  PurchasesFailureReason.alreadyOwned => context.l10n.paywallFailureOwned,
  PurchasesFailureReason.notAllowed => context.l10n.paywallFailureNotAllowed,
  PurchasesFailureReason.unavailable => context.l10n.paywallFailureUnavailable,
  PurchasesFailureReason.storeProblem => context.l10n.paywallFailureStore,
  PurchasesFailureReason.notConfigured => context.l10n.paywallFailureNotSetUp,
  PurchasesFailureReason.unknown => context.l10n.paywallFailureUnknown,
};

/// The text of the app for [notice].
String noticeTextOf(BuildContext context, PaywallNotice notice) =>
    switch (notice) {
      PaywallNotice.pending => context.l10n.paywallPendingNotice,
      PaywallNotice.nothingToRestore => context.l10n.paywallNothingToRestore,
    };
=== lib/features/paywall/paywall_view.dart (paywall) ===
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:my_app/core/l10n/l10n.dart';

import '../../core/purchases/app_purchases.dart';
import 'paywall_card.dart';
import 'paywall_links.dart';
import 'paywall_state.dart';
import 'paywall_texts.dart';
import 'paywall_widgets.dart';

/// What the paywall shows: the card of premium access, what it gives, a
/// card for each plan of the current offer, the button that buys the
/// selected plan with what the plan costs after its intro offer and the
/// terms of the store, and the links that restore purchases and lead to the
/// terms of use and the privacy policy. Its close button is at the top,
/// outside what scrolls, whenever there is a page below to go back to.
///
/// It keeps nothing of the calls themselves: the screen gives it the state
/// of the paywall and takes what the user asks for. So it is the same
/// whichever way the app manages state. It never closes itself after a
/// purchase or a restore: the router closes it once the user has premium
/// access.
class PaywallView extends StatefulWidget {
  /// Creates the view.
  const PaywallView({
    required this.state,
    required this.onSelect,
    required this.onPurchase,
    required this.onRestore,
    required this.onRetry,
    required this.onOpenLink,
    super.key,
  });

  /// The state of the paywall.
  final PaywallState state;

  /// Selects a plan.
  final void Function(Plan plan) onSelect;

  /// Buys the selected plan.
  final VoidCallback onPurchase;

  /// Restores what the store account of the user bought.
  final VoidCallback onRestore;

  /// Loads the plans again, after they did not load.
  final VoidCallback onRetry;

  /// Opens a link of the paywall outside the app.
  final void Function(Uri url) onOpenLink;

  @override
  State<PaywallView> createState() => _PaywallViewState();
}

class _PaywallViewState extends State<PaywallView>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  /// Lets the parts of the paywall rise in one after another, once.
  late final AnimationController _entrance = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1400),
  );

  /// Lets the cards of the plans come in one after another once they are
  /// there.
  late final AnimationController _plansIn = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 700),
  );

  var _started = false;

  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    if (_started) return;
    _started = true;
    // An app that asks for less motion shows the paywall at once.
    if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      _entrance.value = 1;
      _plansIn.value = 1;
    } else {
      // The entrance starts when the paywall is first built. A widget test
      // that starts the app in real time, as with tester.runAsync(), does
      // so once in its file: when a second test of the file starts the app
      // again, the time of this animation runs backwards, and Flutter
      // fails an assertion.
      _entrance.forward();
      if (widget.state.plans != null) _plansIn.value = 1;
    }
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(PaywallView oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.state.plans == null && widget.state.plans != null) {
      // An app that asks for less motion shows the plans at once.
      if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
        _plansIn.value = 1;
      } else {
        _plansIn.forward(from: 0);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _entrance.dispose();
    _plansIn.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final colors = theme.colorScheme;
    final state = widget.state;
    final idle = state.busy == null;
    final selected = state.selected;
    // Whether a page below this one is there to go back to.
    final canClose = ModalRoute.of(context)?.impliesAppBarDismissal ?? false;
    final failure = state.failure;
    final notice = state.notice;
    final terms = paywallTermsUrl();
    final privacy = paywallPrivacyUrl();
    final afterOffer = selected == null
        ? null
        : afterOfferTextOf(context, selected);
    final inset = MediaQuery.paddingOf(context).bottom;
    // On a phone with room for it, the button stays in sight at the bottom
    // while the rest scrolls. On a small phone, or with a large text size,
    // it scrolls with the rest, so that the plans have room.
    final pinned =
        MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height >= 640 &&
        MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(16) <= 24;
    // What the last call ended with, such as a restore that found nothing.
    final message = PaywallMessage(
      text: failure != null
          ? failureTextOf(context, failure.reason)
          : notice != null
          ? noticeTextOf(context, notice)
          : null,
      failed: failure != null,
      hint: failure?.developerHint,
    );
    // What buys: the message, the button and what the selected plan costs
    // after its intro offer. While the plans did not load, there is nothing
    // to buy, and the message is above the links.
    final purchase = state.loadFailure != null
        ? null
        : Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              message,
              PurchaseButton(
                label: purchaseLabelOf(context, selected),
                busy: state.busy == PaywallAction.purchase,
                onPressed: idle && selected != null ? widget.onPurchase : null,
              ),
              if (afterOffer != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                WholeWords(
                  afterOffer,
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: colors.onSurface,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          );
    // What the user agrees to, and the links.
    final details = Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        if (purchase == null) message,
        if (selected != null && state.loadFailure == null) ...[
          WholeWords(
            termsTextOf(context, selected),
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
              color: colors.onSurfaceVariant,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
        ],
        _Links(
          links: [
            // While the plans load, there is no restore either, so that no
            // plan comes in to be bought while one is on its way.
            PaywallLink(
              label: context.l10n.paywallRestore,
              busy: state.busy == PaywallAction.restore,
              onPressed: idle && !state.loading ? widget.onRestore : null,
            ),
            if (terms != null)
              PaywallLink(
                label: context.l10n.paywallTerms,
                onPressed: () => widget.onOpenLink(terms),
              ),
            if (privacy != null)
              PaywallLink(
                label: context.l10n.paywallPrivacy,
                onPressed: () => widget.onOpenLink(privacy),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
    return Scaffold(
      body: SafeArea(
        bottom: false,
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // The way out stays in sight on any phone, outside what
            // scrolls.
            SizedBox(
              height: 52,
              child: Align(
                alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
                child: canClose
                    ? Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsetsDirectional.only(start: 8),
                        child: IconButton(
                          tooltip: context.l10n.paywallClose,
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded),
                          onPressed: () => Navigator.maybePop(context),
                        ),
                      )
                    : null,
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                // Without the button below it, the paywall scrolls under
                // what covers the bottom of the screen, such as the bar of
                // the home gesture, and ends above it.
                padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                  20,
                  0,
                  20,
                  pinned && purchase != null ? 24 : 32 + inset,
                ),
                child: _Phone(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      Rise(
                        animation: _entrance,
                        order: 0,
                        child: PremiumCard(
                          animation: _entrance,
                          premium: context.l10n.paywallPremium,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      Rise(
                        animation: _entrance,
                        order: 1,
                        child: _Benefits(
                          benefits: [
                            context.l10n.paywallBenefitAll,
                            context.l10n.paywallBenefitNew,
                            context.l10n.paywallBenefitSupport,
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      Rise(
                        animation: _entrance,
                        order: 2,
                        child: _plansOf(context, state),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      if (!pinned && purchase != null) ...[
                        Rise(animation: _entrance, order: 3, child: purchase),
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      ],
                      Rise(animation: _entrance, order: 4, child: details),
                      DeveloperNote(
                        hint: state.loadFailure?.developerHint,
                        keys: missingPaywallKeys(),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            if (pinned && purchase != null)
              Rise(
                animation: _entrance,
                order: 3,
                child: DecoratedBox(
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: colors.surface,
                    border: Border(
                      top: BorderSide(color: colors.outlineVariant),
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Padding(
                    // Above what covers the bottom of the screen.
                    padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 12, 20, 12 + inset),
                    child: _Phone(child: purchase),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  /// The plans of [state]: grey cards while they load, a card for each of
  /// them, or what the paywall says when they did not load, with the button
  /// that loads them again.
  Widget _plansOf(BuildContext context, PaywallState state) {
    final plans = state.plans;
    final Widget shown;
    if (plans != null) {
      shown = Column(
        key: const ValueKey('plans'),
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          for (final (index, plan) in plans.indexed) ...[
            if (index > 0) const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Rise(
              animation: _plansIn,
              order: index,
              child: PlanCard(
                name: planNameOf(context, plan),
                price: plan.price,
                suffix: priceSuffixOf(context, plan.period),
                offer: switch (plan.introOffer) {
                  final offer? => offerTextOf(context, offer),
                  null => null,
                },
                badge: isBestValue(plan, plans)
                    ? context.l10n.paywallBestValue
                    : null,
                selected: plan == state.selected,
                onTap: state.busy == null ? () => widget.onSelect(plan) : null,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      );
    } else if (state.loadFailure != null) {
      shown = _Unavailable(
        key: const ValueKey('unavailable'),
        onRetry: widget.onRetry,
      );
    } else {
      shown = const Column(
        key: ValueKey('loading'),
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [PlanSkeleton(), SizedBox(height: 12), PlanSkeleton()],
      );
    }
    if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) return shown;
    return AnimatedSwitcher(
      duration: Durations.medium2,
      // The plans that come in and the skeleton that leaves are as wide
      // as the paywall.
      layoutBuilder: (current, previous) => Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
        fit: StackFit.passthrough,
        children: [...previous, ?current],
      ),
      child: shown,
    );
  }
}
